सार्वजनिक नोटिस 


एनएचपीसी लिमिटेड, जिसका अपना पंजीकृत कार्यालय एनएचपीसी ऑफिस कॉम्प्लेक्स, सेक्टर-33, फरीदाबाद-424003 (हरियाणा) में है, 
भारत सरकार से आवेदन करना चाहता है कि वह उसे विद्युत अधिनियम, 2003 की धारा (64 के अंतर्गत समस्त शक्तियां प्रदान करे, जिससे 
विद्युत के संचरण हेतु विद्युत लाइनें या विद्युत संयंत्र लगाने या टेलीफोन या टेलीग्राफिक संचार के प्रयोजन के लिए आवश्यक कार्यों के समुचित

(Regulation) Rules, 957 for information/public comments/criticism. Any person having any comments/ observations on the

proposed amendments to the Rules & Bye Laws of NCCL may send the same to NCCL at compliance @nccl.co.in within 
fifteen days from the date of this publication. The observation received after the said fifteenth day will not be considered. The 
draft will be taken in to consideration immediately after expiry of fifteen days’. 


A. PROPOSED AMENDMENT TO THE RULES OF THE NATIONAL COMMODITY CLEARING LIMITED 


l. 


Short title and commencement 

(.) This amendment shall be called NCCL Rules (Amendment), 2024. 

(2) It shall come into force with effect from the date of notification in Gazette of India. 

A new sub clause 8.7.!2 is proposed to be inserted after the existing sub clause 8.7.l! under Rule 8.7: 


“8.7.l2 The Clearing Members shall adhere to the standards and guidelines prescribed by NCCL/ SEBI, as the case 
may be, for strengthening cyber resilience and maintaining robust cybersecurity.” 


A new clause 9.26 is proposed to be inserted after the existing clause 9.25 under Rule 9: 
“9.26 RELEVANT AUTHORITY FOR THE PURPOSE OF THIS CHAPTER 


The Relevant Authority for the purpose of this Chapter shall be the Member Committee or any Committee as may 
be termed by SEBI from time to time. The composition, quorum, terms of reference of such Member Committee/ 
Committee shall be such as may be prescribed and notified by SEBI/ Clearing Corporation from time to time.” 


PROPOSED AMENDMENT TO THE BYE LAWS OF THE NATIONAL COMMODITY CLEARING LIMITED 
Short title and commencement 

(.) This amendment shall be called NCCL Bye Laws (Amendment), 2024. 

(2) It shall come into force with effect from the date of notification in Gazette of India. 

A new clause 2.30 is proposed to be inserted after the existing clause !2.29 under Byelaw 2: 

“| 2.30 THE RELEVANT AUTHORITY FOR THE PURPOSE OF THIS CHAPTER 


The Relevant Authority for the purpose of this Chapter shall be the Member Committee or any Committee as may 
be termed by SEBI from time to time.”
